            function getCookieTestPub (cookieName = 'x-ff-abtest-group') {
                const cookies = document.cookie.split(';');
                for (let cookie of cookies) {
                    const cookieInfo = cookie.split('=');
                    if (cookieInfo[0].trim() === cookieName) {
                        return cookieInfo[1].trim();
                    }
                }
                return null;
            }

            if (getCookieTestPub() !== 'A') {
                window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
                const advertisementDatalayerIndex = window.dataLayer.findLastIndex((entry) => !!entry.advertisement);

                let currentAdvertisement = {};
                if (advertisementDatalayerIndex != -1) {
                    currentAdvertisement = window.dataLayer[advertisementDatalayerIndex].advertisement;
                }

                const currentAdvertisementBrand = currentAdvertisement.brand || {};
                currentAdvertisement.brand = {...currentAdvertisementBrand, abbr: 'fac-test'};

                if (advertisementDatalayerIndex != -1) {
                    window.dataLayer[advertisementDatalayerIndex].advertisement = currentAdvertisement;
                } else {
                    window.dataLayer.push({advertisement: currentAdvertisement});

                var brandAbbr = 'fac-test';
